/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   99. Bottom-sheet popups (mobile)
   Every centered popup becomes an Instagram-style sheet that
   slides UP on open and slides DOWN on close. Add a `sheet-closing`
   class (driven by hooks/useSheet.ts) to the backdrop to play the
   exit animation before unmounting.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  @keyframes bsSheetUp {
    from { transform: translateY(105%); }
    to { transform: translateY(0); }
  }

  @keyframes bsSheetDown {
    from { transform: translateY(0); }
    to { transform: translateY(105%); }
  }

  @keyframes bsBackdropIn {
    from { opacity: 0; }
    to { opacity: 1; }
  }

  @keyframes bsBackdropOut {
    from { opacity: 1; }
    to { opacity: 0; }
  }

  /* Backdrops pin the sheet to the bottom */
  .qz-modal-backdrop,
  .cal-modal-overlay,
  .nt-overlay,
  .pf-modal-overlay,
  .ac-overlay,
  .fm-confirm-overlay,
  .flox-backdrop {
    align-items: flex-end !important;
    justify-content: center !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    animation: bsBackdropIn 0.22s ease-out !important;
  }

  /* The sheet itself */
  .qz-modal,
  .cal-modal,
  .nt-modal,
  .pf-modal,
  .ac-modal,
  .fm-confirm,
  .flox-modal {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 100%;
    max-height: 92svh;
    margin: 0;
    border-radius: 20px 20px 0 0;
    overflow-y: auto;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    padding-bottom: calc(1rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
    animation: bsSheetUp 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
  }

  /* The quiz modal keeps its internal scrolling body */
  .qz-modal {
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Grab handle */
  .qz-modal::before,
  .cal-modal::before,
  .nt-modal::before,
  .pf-modal::before,
  .ac-modal::before,
  .fm-confirm::before,
  .flox-modal::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 8px;
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    width: 40px;
    height: 4px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: var(--muted, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28));
    opacity: 0.5;
    pointer-events: none;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  /* Slide-down exit animation */
  .qz-modal-backdrop.sheet-closing,
  .cal-modal-overlay.sheet-closing,
  .nt-overlay.sheet-closing,
  .pf-modal-overlay.sheet-closing,
  .ac-overlay.sheet-closing,
  .fm-confirm-overlay.sheet-closing,
  .flox-backdrop.sheet-closing {
    animation: bsBackdropOut 0.24s ease-in forwards !important;
  }

  .qz-modal-backdrop.sheet-closing > .qz-modal,
  .cal-modal-overlay.sheet-closing > .cal-modal,
  .nt-overlay.sheet-closing > .nt-modal,
  .pf-modal-overlay.sheet-closing > .pf-modal,
  .ac-overlay.sheet-closing > .ac-modal,
  .fm-confirm-overlay.sheet-closing > .fm-confirm,
  .flox-backdrop.sheet-closing > .flox-modal {
    animation: bsSheetDown 0.26s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.9, 0.4) forwards !important;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   APP SHELL  (final authority â€” imported last)
   ------------------------------------------------------------
   The authenticated app behaves like a fixed native app frame at
   every screen size:

     â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”
     â”‚  fixed top bar  (brand/app) â”‚   sticky inside scrollport
     â”œâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”¤
     â”‚  content        (scrolls)   â”‚   overflow-y: auto
     â”‚                             â”‚
     â”œâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”¤
     â”‚  fixed bottom nav (mobile)  â”‚   fixed + safe-area
     â””â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”˜

   The document never scrolls (body.app-shell overflow hidden);
   only the .app-main scrollport scrolls, which keeps the top bar,
   bottom nav and side overlays pinned like Instagram / YouTube.
   ============================================================ */

html {
  height: 100%;
}

/* Lock the frame */
body.app-shell {
  height: 100%;
  height: 100dvh;
  overflow: hidden;
}

/* The single scrollport for all authenticated content */
body.app-shell .app-main {
  height: 100dvh;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
  -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
}

body.app-shell .app-main-public {
  overflow-y: auto;
}

/* Focus Mode full-screen phase: the app must not scroll behind it */
body.app-shell.fm-immersive .app-main {
  overflow: hidden;
}

     Backdrop
       â””â”€â”€ Sheet
           â”œâ”€â”€ Drag handle + Header
